Consider using `scipy.optimize.minimize` on x with objective:
```
def obj_grad(x):
    a = np.exp(np.clip(x, -50, 50)); a/=a.sum()
    b = fftconvolve(a,a)
    # smooth max: 
    M = b.max()
    # softmax weights w = softmax((b - M)/tau) 
    z = (b - M)/tau
    z -= z.max()
    w = np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
    F = M + tau*np.log(w.sum())  # smooth max
    g = correlation(a,w)  # length n: g_i = sum_l a_l w_{i+l}
    # grad F wrt a: 2*g (since F = logsumexp? derivative of logsumexp is ∑w ∂b; ∂b=2 partner)
    # But F=M+tau log sum exp((b-M)/tau), w = exp((b-M)/tau)/sum, derivative =2g.
    # grad wrt x: f_i =2g_i; grad_x = f_j a_j - sum_i f_i a_i
    grad = 2*(a*g - (2*g? wait sum f_i a_i =2∑g_i a_i) times a? Let's derive)
    # f_i = ∂F/∂a_i = 2 g_i.
    # grad_x_j = ∑_i f_i (δ_ij a_i - a_i a_j) = f_j a_j - a_j ∑_i f_i a_i = 2a_j*g_j - 2a_j ∑_i a_i g_i.
    # = 2a_j*(g_j - h), h=∑a_i g_i.
```
Then L-BFGS-B minimizes smooth max with sum=1. Bounds x maybe [-40,40]. At end, evaluate exact score. This is a natural optimization.

We can implement a robust local descent in log domain using exact objective (max) with active weights and backtracking. This may be faster than scipy L-BFGS. Steps:
- Normalize sum=1.
- Compute b via FFT/full. M= max b. score =2nM.
- Choose weights w: if using exact max, weights active = 1/count at M - tol. Smooth with temperature for gradient.
- Compute g = correlation(a,w). Since b_k symmetric, w length 2n-1.
- Direction in a: δ = g - mean(g) (to decrease? Gradient f=2g. Projected gradient for minimization under sum=1 is δ_a = -g + mean(g) = mean(g)-g). In log x, direction d_x = g - mean_a_weighted? Need choose. For additive in a: a_new = a + step*(mean(g)-g), clip nonneg, normalize. For log multiplicative: a_new = a * exp(step*(mean_weighted? maybe h?)). Let's derive log with sum normalization automatically if use x update d = 2a*(g - h) where h=∑a g. This is gradient of F wrt x. If we take x ← x - η grad_x, then a changes multiplicatively: a_i ← a_i * exp(-η 2a_i(g_i-h)). This suggests decrease coordinates with large a_i g_i. But additive direction may be better for creating zeros. We can use both.

Projection in a: δ=mean(g)-g. Note g_i≥0. Sum δ maybe not zero if we subtract arithmetic mean; yes sum δ=0. But a+δ may negative. Use line search reduce step. If min a+δ <0, step *= min(a / -δ neg). Then normalize. Evaluate exact. Accept if score improves. If no improvement, perturb/random.

```
def correlate(a,w):
    n=len(a); L=len(w)
    N=1 << (n+len(w)-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    B=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],N)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)
    return corr[L-1:L-1-n:-1]? Need careful with slices.
```
We want m from L-1 down to L-n. In Python, `corr[L-1-n+1 : L+1][::-1]` => start L-n, stop L+1, reverse. Example L=3,n=2: start1, stop4 -> indices1,2 reversed -> [2,1], ok.
```
return corr[L-n:L+1][::-1]
```

Implementation of FFT convolution:
```
def fft_convolve(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    N=1 << (n+m-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:n+m-1]
```
For self-conv, maybe use A*A. For a normalized length n, if n=4806, N=16384? n+m-1=9611, next power 16384. Good.

       g=correlate(a,w)

Let's implement `fast_score(a)`:
```
def fast_score(a):
   b=fft_convolve(a,a); b=np.max(b); s=a.sum()
   return 2*len(a)*b/(s*s)
```

Need ensure if arrays length small, N power of two. `A = np.fft.rfft(a, n)`, `B = np.fft.rfft(b, n)`, `out = np.fft.irfft(A*B, n)[:n+m-1]`. For self-conv, `A*A`. N2 = next power of 3n-2, which is >= N1. We can use same N = next power of max(2n-1,3n-2)=3n-2 for both. For n=4806, N=16384. Then b = irfft(A_two? We need A at N. Compute A_N once, b=irfft(A_N*A_N)[:2n-1], g_corr=irfft(A_N * Wrev_N)[:3n-2], extract. Good. This saves one FFT.
```
def fast_b_and_grad(a,w):
   n=len(a); L=2n-1; N=1<<(3*n-2).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
   b=np.maximum(b,0.)
   Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],N)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(A*Wr,N)
   g=corr[L-n:L+1][::-1]
   # g may small errors, clip? g should nonnegative; set small negative 0
   g=np.maximum(g,0.)
   return b,g
```
Check slice: L=2n-1. corr length N. corr indices L-n to L inclusive. For n=4806, L-n=n-1, ok.
If n is small, N maybe? n=1, 3n-2=1, bit_length 1? `1 << (1).bit_length()` gives2? (1).bit_length=1, 1<<1=2; ok. Could just `N=1; while N < 3*n-2: N*=2`.

But for line search with trial a, we don't need g, only b. Use `fast_score(a)` with A_N for 2n-1. We can compute with `N=next_power(2n-1)`.

def recurrence_sequence(n):
    q=np.empty(n); q[0]=1.0
    if n>1:
       i=np.arange(1,n, dtype=float)
       ratios=(2*i-1)/(2*i)
       q[1:]=np.cumprod(ratios)
    return q
```

Random perturbations details:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng):
    n=len(a)
    c=a.copy()
    # block multiplicative
    width = max(1, n//100)
    start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
    c[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2))
    if rng.random()<0.3: c=0.5*(c+c[::-1])
    c=np.clip(c,0,1000); s=c.sum(); if s>0: c/=s
    return c
```

```
def eval_fast_trial(a, N):
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
   b[b<0]=0
   s=a.sum() # should 1
   return 2*len(a)*b.max()/(s*s)
```

Safe step function:
```
def trial_from(a, delta, alpha):
    # delta = g - mean(g), we subtract alpha*delta
    if alpha <=0: return None
    a_try = a - alpha*delta
    if np.any(a_try < 0):
        pos_mask = delta > 0
        if not np.any(pos_mask): return None? Actually if delta>0, a_try negative when a < alpha delta.
        max_safe = np.min(a[pos_mask] / delta[pos_mask])
        alpha = max(0.0, min(alpha, 0.7*max_safe))
        if alpha ==0: return None
        a_try = a - alpha*delta
    # clip tiny negatives and >1000
    a_try = np.clip(a_try, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = a_try.sum()
    if s <= 0: return None
    a_try /= s
    return a_try
```
If delta has positive where a very small, safe alpha may be tiny; grid alphas may all become same small. But if safe tiny, direction wants to reduce a coordinate more than its size; step limited. Good.

But if a_try after clip has some zeros, nonneg. Normalization may slightly change direction. Fine.

Could solve linear system C_K g = M. For current a=q, active first n. This is exactly the recurrence? Let's see: If a=q, C_K g = 1 for k=0..n-1. This linear system solves g coefficients such that q*g first n =1. The solution might be g = q? Since q*q first n=1, yes q is a solution. Could use "curvature" direction: Since active first n all active, first-order feasible directions have ∑ a_i a_{k-i} δ_i =0 for k<n. This is a linear system. The nullspace dimension n (since n constraints, n variables) might be trivial if C invertible (C is lower triangular Toeplitz with a0>0). def _fft_b_max(a, N, L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    if b.size:
       bmax=float(b.max())
       if bmax<0: bmax=0
       return bmax
    return 0.0
```

Detailed optimizer code:
```
def _optimize(a, limit_s, seed, max_iter=200000):
    a = np.asarray(a, float).copy()
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=0: return None
    a /= s
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=_pow2(3*n-2)
    start=time.monotonic()
    cur_score=2*n*_b_max(a,N,L)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    base=1.0
    stall=0
    it=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time.monotonic()-start < limit_s and it < max_iter:
        it +=1
        b,g,M = _b_and_weights(a, tau_frac, N,L)
        if M <=0: break
        old_score=2*n*M
        # update best fast
        if old_score < best_score:
            best_score=old_score; best_a=a.copy()
        if M < best_score/(2*n): # no
        delta = g - np.mean(g)
        dmax = delta.max()-delta.min()
        if dmax < 1e-18:
            # random kick
        # alphas: [base, base*0.5, base*2.0]
        best_trial=None; best_trial_score=old_score
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
            if time > start+limit? check
            trial = _trial(a, delta, base*mult)
            if trial is None: continue
            Mtry=_b_max(trial,N,L)
            score=2*n*Mtry
            if finite and score < best_trial_score - 1e-15:
                best_trial=trial; best_trial_score=score
        if best_trial is not None:
            a=best_trial; base=min(base*1.3, 20.0); stall=0
        else:
            base=max(base*0.8, 0.05); stall+=1
            if stall > 80:
                # random perturb
                for _ in range(5):
                    trial = _random_trial(a, rng)
                    if trial:
                       score=2*n*_b_max(trial,N,L)
                       if score < old_score:
                          a=trial; best...; base=1; stall=0; break
                if best random: continue? else break? If stall > 500: return best_a
    return best_a
```

`_trial`: Use `delta` and alpha. Need if alpha extremely large due base 20, safe. We'll create safe alpha:
```
def _safe_trial(a, delta, alpha):
    if alpha <=0 or not finite: return None
    a_try = a - alpha*delta
    if (a_try < 0).any():
        mask = delta > 0
        if mask.any():
            safe = np.min(a[mask]/delta[mask])
            alpha = min(alpha, 0.7*safe)
        if alpha <=0: return None
        a_try = a - alpha*delta
    a_try = np.where(a_try > 0, a_try, 0.0)
    s=a_try.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return None
    a_try /= s
    return a_try
```
Since delta = g - mean(g), if all delta<=0? Then a_try all >=a, no neg, sum >1 normalize. Fine.

    t=np.arange(n)/(n-1)

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_global=time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_global + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_arr = None; best_score = inf
    def exact_update(arr):
       nonlocal best_arr,best_score
       ... evaluate_sequence
    # Determine n
    n = 4806
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0 and np.isfinite(p).all():
          parent = np.clip(p,0,1000)
          ps=parent.sum()
          if ps>0.01:
             parent = parent/ps
             if 1000 <= parent.size <= 8000: n = 4806  # or parent.size
             exact_update(parent)
          if p.size>0 and p.size <= 6000:
             n = int(p.size)
    except NameError: parent=None
    # cap n
    if n < 64: n=4806
    if n > 6000: n=4806
    rec = recurrence_normalized(n)
    exact_update(rec)
    # generate variations and maybe optimize small
    inits=[]
    inits.append(rec)
    inits.append(rec[::-1])
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for beta in (-0.5,-0.2,-0.1,0.1,0.2,0.5):
        c=rec*(1+beta*t)
        if c.min()>=0: inits.append(c/ c.sum())
        c2=rec*(1+beta*(1-t)**2)
        if c2.min()>=0: inits.append(c2/c2.sum())
    if parent is not None and parent.size==n: inits.append(parent)
    # fast select top inits (maybe 3)
    Nsel=_pow2(2*n-1); L=2*n-1
    scored=[]
    for arr in inits:
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000); s=arr.sum(); if s<=0: continue
       arr=arr/s
       sc=2*n*_b_max(arr,Nsel,L)
       if finite: scored.append((sc,arr))
    scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    # exact update top few if fast below best_score
    for sc,arr in scored[:5]:
       if sc < best_score - 1e-7:
          exact_update(arr)
    # primary init = best exact arr if fast among top? Use scored[0] if any else rec

    # If parent exact better, maybe use parent as init? We can optimize both rec and parent? Let's choose init with lowest exact among those not too high? For final optimize, use the best fast init, but if exact best differs (parent), maybe optimize parent later.
    # Run optimizer on init

       limit = min(600, max(30, (deadline - time.monotonic())*0.6))
       opt1 = _optimize(init, limit, seed)

    # If parent exists and different, optimize parent with remaining 300s

Let's implement `_b_and_weights`:
```
def _b_and_weights(a, frac, N, L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    if b.size==0: return b, None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): b=np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    M=float(b.max())
    if M <=0:
       w=np.zeros(L); if L>0: w[0]=1
       return b,w,M
    b[b<0]=0.0
    tau=max(M*frac, 1e-300)
    z=(b-M)/tau
    # clip z to [-700,0] to avoid overflow? z max 0, min negative.
    z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: idx=int(np.argmax(b)); w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0
    else: w/=s
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(A*Wr, N)
    g=corr[L-(len(a)-1?):]
```
Need n inside; pass n. `start=L-n+1`? We want indices L-n+1? Earlier `L-n` to `L` inclusive. For n=4806,L=9611, start=4805, stop=9612; length=4807? Wait L+1 - (L-n) = n+1? Let's recalc. We need indices m = L-1 - i for i=0..n-1: max m=L-1, min m=L-n. That's n indices from L-n to L-1 inclusive. Python slice `corr[L-n : L]` gives start L-n, stop L (exclusive) -> length n. Then reverse: `corr[L-n:L][::-1]`. Earlier I used `L+1` which gives n+1. Let's test n=2,L=3: indices m=2,1. Slice start1 stop3 -> indices1,2; reverse [2,1]. Good. So `g = corr[L-n:L][::-1]`. For n=1,L=1: start0 stop1 -> index0. Good. Slight correction.

Random perturb function using FFT score inside optimizer:
```
def _random_perturb(c, rng, n):
   r = c.copy()
   mode = rng.integers(0,5)
   if mode==0:
      w = max(1,n//100); st=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1)); r[st:st+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3))
   elif mode==1:
      k=max(1,n//50); r[-k:] *= 1.3; r[:k] *= 0.8
   elif mode==2:
      k=max(1,n//50); r[:k] *= 1.3; r[-k:] *= 0.8
   elif mode==3:
      r = 0.5*(r+r[::-1])
   else:
      sigma=0.05*c.mean(); r += rng.normal(0,sigma,c.size)
   r=np.clip(r,0,1000); s=r.sum(); if s<=0: return None; r/=s; return r
```

```
def _b_and_weights(a, frac, N, L):
    n = a.size
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:L]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    M = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
       w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float); w[0]=1.0; g = np.zeros(n); return b,g,M
    tau = max(M*frac, 1e-300)
    z = (b - M)/tau
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(b)); w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0
    else: w /= s
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(A*Wr, N)
    start = L - n
    g = corr[start:L][::-1]
    g = np.maximum(g,0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return b,g,M
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_b_max(a: np.ndarray, N: int, L: int) -> float:
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    if b.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 0.0
    return max(0.0, m)


def _b_and_weights(a: np.ndarray, frac: float, N: int, L: int):
    """
    Return:
      b = full convolution a*a (length L = 2*n-1, FFT approx)
      g = gradient helper g_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}
      M = max b
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

    M = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        if L:
            w[0] = 1.0
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return b, g, M

    tau = max(M * frac, 1e-300)
    z = (b - M) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(b))
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s

    Wr = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(A * Wr, N)

    start = L - n
    g = corr[start:L][::-1].copy()
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    return b, g, M


def _safe_trial(a: np.ndarray, delta: np.ndarray, alpha: float):
    if alpha <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(alpha):
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
        return None

    trial = a - alpha * delta
    if np.any(trial < 0.0):
        mask = delta > 0.0
        if np.any(mask):
            safe = float(np.min(a[mask] / delta[mask]))
            if not np.isfinite(safe) or safe <= 0.0:
                return None
            alpha = min(alpha, 0.7 * safe)
            if alpha <= 0.0:
                return None
            trial = a - alpha * delta

    trial = np.clip(trial, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(trial.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    trial /= s
    return trial


def _random_perturb(c: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator):
    n = int(c.size)
    r = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))

    if mode == 0:
        width = max(1, n // 100)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        r[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.30)))
    elif mode == 1:
        k = max(1, n // 50)
        r[-k:] *= 1.30
        r[:k] *= 0.80
    elif mode == 2:
        k = max(1, n // 50)
        r[:k] *= 1.30
        r[-k:] *= 0.80
    elif mode == 3:
        r = 0.5 * (r + r[::-1])
    else:
        sigma = 0.05 * float(np.mean(r))
        r = r + rng.normal(0.0, max(sigma, 1e-12), size=n)

    r = np.clip(r, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
        return None
    s = float(r.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    r /= s
    return r


def _recurrence_sequence(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n.
    This makes the first n convolution coefficients equal to 1 for the
    unnormalized sequence, a strong nonconstant initialization.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    q = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    q[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        i = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64)
        ratios = (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
        q[1:] = np.cumprod(ratios)
    return np.maximum(q, 0.0)


def _optimize_one(a0: np.ndarray, limit_s: float, seed: int, max_iter: int = 200000):
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    a /= s

    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(3 * n - 2)

    start = time.monotonic()
    M0 = _fft_b_max(a, N, L)
    cur_score = 2.0 * n * M0
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur_score

    base = 1.0
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        return best_a

    while time.monotonic() - start < limit_s and it < max_iter:
        it += 1

        # Broader early, sharper later.  If stalled, temporarily broaden again.
        frac = 0.015 + 0.50 * (0.5 ** (it / 700.0))
        if stall > 80:
            frac = max(frac, 0.35)

        b, g, M = _b_and_weights(a, frac, N, L)
        if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        old_score = 2.0 * n * M
        if old_score < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = old_score
            best_a = a.copy()

        delta = g - float(np.mean(g))
        spread = float(delta.max() - delta.min()) if delta.size else 0.0

        accepted = False

        if spread > 1e-18:
            best_trial = None
            best_trial_score = old_score
            for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                if time.monotonic() - start > limit_s:
                    break
                trial = _safe_trial(a, delta, base * mult)
                if trial is None:
                    continue
                Mtry = _fft_b_max(trial, N, L)
                sc = 2.0 * n * Mtry
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score - 1e-15:
                    best_trial_score = sc
                    best_trial = trial
            if best_trial is not None:
                a = best_trial
                cur_score = best_trial_score
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best_score = cur_score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                base = min(base * 1.35, 16.0)
                stall = 0
                accepted = True
        else:
            stall += 1

        if not accepted:
            base = max(base * 0.82, 0.04)
            stall += 1

            if stall > 100:
                for _ in range(8):
                    if time.monotonic() - start > limit_s:
                        break
                    trial = _random_perturb(a, rng)
                    if trial is None:
                        continue
                    Mtry = _fft_b_max(trial, N, L)
                    sc = 2.0 * n * Mtry
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < old_score - 1e-15:
                        a = trial
                        cur_score = sc
                        if sc < best_score:
                            best_score = sc
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        base = 1.0
                        stall = 0
                        break

            if stall > 1200:
                break

    return best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 10.0)

    best_arr = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def exact_update(arr):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_score
        if arr is None:
            return
        try:
            a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            return
        if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            return
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_arr = a.copy()

    # ------------- read optional parent, but never paste its values -------------
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.size <= 7000:
            parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            ps = float(parent.sum())
            if ps > 0.01:
                parent = parent / ps
                exact_update(parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # ---------- choose a practical resolution.  4806 is evaluator-friendly. ----------
    n_final = 4806
    if parent is not None and 512 <= int(parent.size) <= 6000:
        n_final = int(parent.size)
    n_final = int(max(64, min(n_final, 6000)))

    # ---------- robust analytic initializer: truncated (1-z)^(-1/2) ----------
    rec_raw = _recurrence_sequence(n_final)
    rec_sum = float(rec_raw.sum())
    rec_norm = rec_raw / rec_sum if rec_sum > 0.0 else rec_raw.copy()

    exact_update(rec_raw)
    exact_update(rec_norm)

    # ---------- a few cheap shape variants for better restart ----------
    inits = []
    inits.append(rec_norm)
    inits.append(rec_norm[::-1].copy())

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_final, dtype=np.float64)
    for beta in (-0.5, -0.2, -0.1, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5):
        c1 = rec_norm * (1.0 + beta * t)
        c2 = rec_norm * (1.0 + beta * (1.0 - t) * (1.0 - t))
        for c in (c1, c2):
            c = np.clip(c, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
                cs = float(c.sum())
                if cs > 0.0:
                    inits.append(c / cs)
    if parent is not None and int(parent.size) == n_final:
        inits.append(parent.copy())

    scored_inits = []
    Nsel = _pow2(2 * n_final - 1)
    Lsel = 2 * n_final - 1
    seen = 0
    for arr in inits:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        if seen > 24:
            break
        seen += 1
        if arr is None:
            continue
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size != n_final:
            continue
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            continue
        arr = arr / s
        sc = 2.0 * n_final * _fft_b_max(arr, Nsel, Lsel)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored_inits.append((sc, arr))

    scored_inits.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    for sc, arr in scored_inits[:6]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        # Only spend exact convolutions when the fast surrogate looks promising.
        if sc < best_score - 1e-8:
            exact_update(arr)

    init = scored_inits[0][1] if scored_inits else rec_norm

    # ---------- main local refinement on the best large initializer ----------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        limit1 = min(720.0, max(30.0, 0.55 * remaining))
        opt1 = _optimize_one(init, limit1, int(seed), max_iter=30000)
        exact_update(opt1)

    # ---------- if the parent has the same length, try it too ----------
    if (
        parent is not None
        and int(parent.size) == n_final
        and time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0
    ):
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        limit2 = min(360.0, max(20.0, 0.35 * remaining))
        opt2 = _optimize_one(parent, limit2, int(seed) + 9173, max_iter=25000)
        exact_update(opt2)

    # ---------- short exact-safe random refinement of the current best ----------
    if best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        cur = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        n = int(cur.size)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        N = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 555)
        local_score = best_score
        exact_calls = 0

        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 6.0:
            progressed = False
            for _ in range(6):
                trial = _random_perturb(cur, rng)
                if trial is None:
                    continue
                sc_fast = 2.0 * n * _fft_b_max(trial, N, L)
                if np.isfinite(sc_fast) and sc_fast < local_score - 1e-7:
                    # Confirm with the exact evaluator only on apparent improvements.
                    sc = float("inf")
                    try:
                        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(trial.tolist()))
                    except Exception:
                        sc = float("inf")
                    exact_calls += 1
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_arr = trial
                        local_score = sc
                        cur = trial.copy()
                        progressed = True
                        break
                    if exact_calls > 250:
                        break
            if exact_calls > 300:
                break
            if progressed:
                continue
            # If no promising perturbation is found quickly, stop.
            break

    # ---------- final validation / fallback ----------
    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.where(out > 0.0, out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return [1.0, 1.0]

    # One last exact check on the exact object being returned.
    try:
        final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out]))
        if not np.isfinite(final_sc):
            out = np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        out = np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
